SDK\r\n- Use **Microsoft Agent Framework** for agent app, with following highlights\r\n  - Flexible Agent Framework: build, orchestrate, and deploy AI agents and multi-agent systems\r\n  - Multi-Agent Orchestration: Group chat, sequential, concurrent, and handoff patterns\r\n  - Plugin Ecosystem: Extend with native functions, OpenAPI, Model Context Protocol (MCP), and more\r\n  - LLM Support: OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Azure AI Foundry, and more\r\n  - Runtime Support: In-process and distributed agent execution\r\n  -             "text": "### Microsoft Agent Framework code samples (Python)\r\n\r\n#### Quick Start\r\nConnect GitHub model first, then create the agent instance:\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom agent_framework import ChatAgent\r\nfrom agent_framework.openai import OpenAIChatClient\r\nfrom openai import AsyncOpenAI\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start() -> None:\r\n    openaiClient=AsyncOpenAI(\r\n        base_url=\"https://models.github.ai/inference\",\r\n        api_key=\"<GITHUB_TOKEN>\",\r\n    )\r\n    chatClient=OpenAIChatClient(\r\n        async_client=openaiClient,\r\n        ai_model_id=\"<model-id>\"\r\n    )\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n    )\r\n    \r\n    result=await agent.run(\"hello\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # Hello! How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

SDK\r\n- Use **Microsoft Agent Framework** for agent app, with following highlights\r\n  - Flexible Agent Framework: build, orchestrate, and deploy AI agents and multi-agent systems\r\n  - Multi-Agent Orchestration: Group chat, sequential, concurrent, and handoff patterns\r\n  - Plugin Ecosystem: Extend with native functions, OpenAPI, Model Context Protocol (MCP), and more\r\n  - LLM Support: OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Azure AI Foundry, and more\r\n  - Runtime Support: In-process and distributed agent execution\r\n  - If not specified, use the default one \"gpt-4.1-mini\".\r\n\r\n## Compare GitHub Models in AI Toolkit\r\n- **Do this if user wants to compare two models via chating side-by-side.             "text": "### Microsoft Agent Framework code samples (Python)\r\n\r\n#### Quick Start\r\nConnect GitHub model first, then create the agent instance:\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom agent_framework import ChatAgent\r\nfrom agent_framework.openai import OpenAIChatClient\r\nfrom openai import AsyncOpenAI\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start() -> None:\r\n    openaiClient=AsyncOpenAI(\r\n        base_url=\"https://models.github.ai/inference\",\r\n        api_key=\"<GITHUB_TOKEN>\",\r\n    )\r\n    chatClient=OpenAIChatClient(\r\n        async_client=openaiClient,\r\n        ai_model_id=\"<model-id>\"\r\n    )\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n    )\r\n    \r\n    result=await agent.run(\"hello\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # Hello! How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

SDK\r\n- Use **Microsoft Agent Framework** for agent app, with following highlights\r\n  - Flexible Agent Framework: build, orchestrate, and deploy AI agents and multi-agent systems\r\n  - Multi-Agent Orchestration: Group chat, sequential, concurrent, and handoff patterns\r\n  - Plugin Ecosystem: Extend with native functions, OpenAPI, Model Context Protocol (MCP), and more\r\n  - LLM Support: OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Azure AI Foundry, and more\r\n  - Runtime Support: In-process and distributed agent execution\r\n  - How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

Fallback to simulated weather if no API key is provided\\n        return get_weather_simulated(location)\\n    \\n    try:\\n        # OpenWeatherMap API endpoint\\n        url = f\\\"http://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather\\\"\\n        params = {\\n            \\\"q\\\": location,\\n            \\\"appid\\\": api_key,\\n            \\\"units\\\": \\\"metric\\\"  # Extract weather information\\n        temperature = data[\\\"main\\\"][\\\"temp\\\"]\\n        feels_like = data[\\\"main\\\"][\\\"feels_like\\\"]\\n        humidity = data[\\\"main\\\"][\\\"humidity\\\"]\\n        description = data[\\\"weather\\\"][0][\\\"description\\\"]\\n        city_name = data[\\\"name\\\"]\\n        country = data[\\\"sys\\\"][\\\"country\\\"]\\n        \\n        return (\\n            f\\\"Current weather in {city_name}, {country}:\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"Temperature: {temperature}°C (feels like {feels_like}°C)\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"Conditions: {description.title()}\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"Humidity: {humidity}%\\\"\\n        )\\n        \\n    except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:\\n        return f\\\"Error fetching weather data for {location}: {str(e)}\\\"\\n    except KeyError as e:\\n        return f\\\"Error parsing weather data for {location}. If weather data is unavailable for a location, suggest similar nearby cities or ask for clarification\\n\\nAlways format weather information in a clear, easy-to-read manner.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n            tools=[weather_function, get_weather_forecast],\\n        )\\n    \\n    async def get_weather(self, location: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Get weather information for a specific location.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            location: The city or location to get weather for.\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            Weather information as a string.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        query = f\\\"What's the weather like in {location}?\\\"\\n        result = await self.agent.run(query)\\n        return result.text\\n    \\n    async def chat(self, message: str, thread=None) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Have a conversation with the weather agent.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            message: The user's message.\\n            thread: Optional thread for maintaining conversation context.\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            The agent's response.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        result = await self.agent.run(message, thread=thread)\\n        return result.text\\n    \\n    def get_new_thread(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Create a new conversation thread for maintaining context.\\n        \\n        Returns:\\n            A new thread object.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self.agent.get_new_thread()\\n\\n\\nasync def main():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Example usage of the Weather Agent.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    print(\\\"🌤️ Weather Agent Starting...\\\\n\\\")\\n    \\n    try:\\n        # Type 'quit' to exit.\\\\n\\\")\\n        \\n        while True:\\n            try:\\n                user_input = input(\\\"You: \\\").strip()\\n                if user_input.lower() in ['quit', 'exit', 'bye']:\\n                    print(\\\"Goodbye! 👋\\\")\\n                    break\\n                \\n                if user_input:\\n                    response = await agent.chat(user_input, thread=thread)\\n                    print(f\\\"Weather Agent: {response}\\\\n\\\")\\n                    \\n            except KeyboardInterrupt:\\n                print(\\\"\\\\nGoodbye! 👋\\\")\\n                break\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                print(f\\\"Error: {e}\\\\n\\\")\\n    \\n    except Exception as e:\\n        print(f\\\"Failed to initialize Weather Agent: {e}\\\")\\n        print(\\\"\\\\nMake sure you have:\\\")\\n        print(\\\"1. How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

SDK\r\n- Use **Microsoft Agent Framework** for agent app, with following highlights\r\n  - Flexible Agent Framework: build, orchestrate, and deploy AI agents and multi-agent systems\r\n  - Multi-Agent Orchestration: Group chat, sequential, concurrent, and handoff patterns\r\n  - Plugin Ecosystem: Extend with native functions, OpenAPI, Model Context Protocol (MCP), and more\r\n  - LLM Support: OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Azure AI Foundry, and more\r\n  - Runtime Support: In-process and distributed agent execution\r\n  -             "text": "### Microsoft Agent Framework code samples (Python)\r\n\r\n#### Quick Start\r\nConnect GitHub model first, then create the agent instance:\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom agent_framework import ChatAgent\r\nfrom agent_framework.openai import OpenAIChatClient\r\nfrom openai import AsyncOpenAI\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start() -> None:\r\n    openaiClient=AsyncOpenAI(\r\n        base_url=\"https://models.github.ai/inference\",\r\n        api_key=\"<GITHUB_TOKEN>\",\r\n    )\r\n    chatClient=OpenAIChatClient(\r\n        async_client=openaiClient,\r\n        ai_model_id=\"<model-id>\"\r\n    )\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n    )\r\n    \r\n    result=await agent.run(\"hello\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # Hello! How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

Fallback to simulated weather if no API key is provided\\n        return get_weather_simulated(location)\\n    \\n    try:\\n        # OpenWeatherMap API endpoint\\n        url = f\\\"http://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather\\\"\\n        params = {\\n            \\\"q\\\": location,\\n            \\\"appid\\\": api_key,\\n            \\\"units\\\": \\\"metric\\\"  # Use the available weather tools to get current weather information\\n2. Provide clear, helpful responses about temperature, conditions, and other relevant details\\n3. If asked about forecasts, use the forecast tool\\n4. Be friendly and conversational in your responses\\n5. If weather data is unavailable for a location, suggest similar nearby cities or ask for clarification\\n\\nAlways format weather information in a clear, easy-to-read manner.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n            tools=[weather_function, get_weather_forecast],\\n        )\\n    \\n    async def get_weather(self, location: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Get weather information for a specific location.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            location: The city or location to get weather for.\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            Weather information as a string.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        query = f\\\"What's the weather like in {location}?\\\"\\n        result = await self.agent.run(query)\\n        return result.text\\n    \\n    async def chat(self, message: str, thread=None) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Have a conversation with the weather agent.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            message: The user's message.\\n            thread: Optional thread for maintaining conversation context.\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            The agent's response.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        result = await self.agent.run(message, thread=thread)\\n        return result.text\\n    \\n    def get_new_thread(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Create a new conversation thread for maintaining context.\\n        \\n        Returns:\\n            A new thread object.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self.agent.get_new_thread()\\n\\n\\nasync def main():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Example usage of the Weather Agent.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    print(\\\"🌤️ Weather Agent Starting...\\\\n\\\")\\n    \\n    try:\\n        # Create weather agent (set use_real_weather=False for simulated data)\\n        agent = WeatherAgent(use_real_weather=False)  # Change to True for real weather\\n        \\n        # Create a conversation thread\\n        thread = agent.get_new_thread()\\n        \\n        # Example weather queries\\n        test_queries = [\\n            \\\"What's the weather like in London?\\\",\\n            \\\"How's the weather in Tokyo today?\\\",\\n            \\\"Can you give me a 5-day forecast for New York?\\\",\\n            \\\"What about the weather in a small town like Palo Alto?\\\",\\n        ]\\n        \\n        for query in test_queries:\\n            print(f\\\"User: {query}\\\")\\n            response = await agent.chat(query, thread=thread)\\n            print(f\\\"Agent: {response}\\\\n\\\")\\n            print(\\\"-\\\" * 50 + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n        \\n        # Interactive mode\\n        print(\\\"🌤️ Weather Agent is ready! Type 'quit' to exit.\\\\n\\\")\\n        \\n        while True:\\n            try:\\n                user_input = input(\\\"You: \\\").strip()\\n                if user_input.lower() in ['quit', 'exit', 'bye']:\\n                    print(\\\"Goodbye! 👋\\\")\\n                    break\\n                \\n                if user_input:\\n                    response = await agent.chat(user_input, thread=thread)\\n                    print(f\\\"Weather Agent: {response}\\\\n\\\")\\n                    \\n            except KeyboardInterrupt:\\n                print(\\\"\\\\nGoodbye! 👋\\\")\\n                break\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                print(f\\\"Error: {e}\\\\n\\\")\\n    \\n    except Exception as e:\\n        print(f\\\"Failed to initialize Weather Agent: {e}\\\")\\n        print(\\\"\\\\nMake sure you have:\\\")\\n        print(\\\"1.
